On hard math, at matched training FLOPs, it more than doubles GRPO's accuracy on held-out problems from the training distribution for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x on Qwen3-1.7B and 1.7x on AIME, while roughly halving trace length. The method is implemented in data preparation plus a loss mask on prefix tokens; the trainer is otherwise stock. The smaller the model, the larger the gain.

</details>


### [106] [How Data Shapes RoPE Frequency Usage: From Positional Scale Matching to Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07678)
*Xinyi Wu,Siyuan Liu,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoPE频率的选择是为了匹配训练数据的相对距离结构，最优频率与数据依赖宽度成反比(1/W)；长上下文泛化依赖于频率与依赖结构的双重尺度匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE为Transformer提供固定的位置频率网格，但训练模型对这些频率的使用高度不均匀。本文旨在探究决定这种频率使用模式的因素，从数据为中心的视角解释频率选择机制。

Method: 将每个RoPE频率视为"位置透镜"，形式化视野-分辨率权衡关系；通过合成数据和文本数据的对照实验，验证频率匹配原则（最优频率与数据依赖宽度W成反比）；将频率选择与位置插值方法建立理论联系。

Result: 1) 证明了频率匹配原则：最优频率与数据依赖宽度W成反比(1/W)；2) 解释了语言模型中中低频带使用源于自然语言多尺度依赖结构；3) 发现自然语言在位置尺度上具有近似自相似性；4) 揭示了长上下文泛化依赖于双重尺度匹配：学习频率与训练依赖的匹配，以及频率缩放与依赖扩展方式的匹配。

Conclusion: RoPE频率使用是数据驱动的涌现现象，频率选择反映训练数据的依赖结构；长上下文泛化的成功取决于两种尺度匹配：学习频率与训练时依赖的匹配，以及频率缩放与依赖随上下文长度扩展方式的匹配。这为理解和改进长上下文泛化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) provide transformers with a fixed grid of positional frequencies, yet trained models use these frequencies highly non-uniformly. We study what determines this frequency usage and propose a data-centered explanation: RoPE frequencies are selected to match the relative-distance structure of the training data. Viewing each frequency as a positional lens, we formalize a field-resolution tradeoff and show that, for a data-induced dependency profile of width $W$, the optimal frequency scales as $1/W$. This frequency-matching principle explains controlled observations on synthetic and text-based data, and suggests that the mid-low frequency bands observed in language models arise from the multi-scale dependency structure of natural language. We further connect frequency selection to position-interpolation-based length generalization: scaling frequencies down expands the effective field while reducing resolution. This helps when longer-context dependencies are approximate dilations of those seen during training, but can fail when relevant dependencies do not scale with context length. Empirically, we show that natural language exhibits approximate self-similarity across positional scales, explaining why test-time frequency scaling can support long-context generalization. Overall, our results identify a data-driven mechanism behind emergent RoPE frequency usage and show that long-context generalization depends on two forms of scale matching: between learned frequencies and training-time dependencies, and between frequency scaling and how those dependencies extend to longer contexts.

</details>


### [107] [Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy for PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07682)
*Xiangming Huang,Guannan Zhang,Lu Lu,Raphaël Pestourie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NOTES框架，通过结合DeepONet神经算子与CMA-ES进化策略，在高维偏微分方程物理系统的逆设计问题上实现了高效、可迁移的优化，并在纳米光子学和结构优化任务上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程控制的物理系统逆设计问题面临设计空间高维度和非凸性的双重挑战。现有的生成模型方法缺乏鲁棒性和可迁移性，而进化策略虽然鲁棒但在高维空间中表现不佳。因此需要一种能够结合两者优势、实现高效降维和全局优化的新方法。

Method: 提出了Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy (NOTES)框架。该方法将基于DeepONet的神经算子与协方差矩阵自适应进化策略(CMA-ES)相结合，在紧凑的潜在空间中执行全局优化。核心创新在于：利用DeepONet学习拓扑感知的先验知识，实现从高维设计空间到低维潜在空间的映射；在潜在空间中应用CMA-ES进行全局优化；将拓扑学习与PDE求解器中的物理控制解耦，提高了框架的灵活性和可迁移性。

Result: 在纳米光子束偏转器逆设计任务（由Maxwell方程控制）中，NOTES将设计维度从256降至25，持续实现超过95%的效率，性能优于CMA-ES、拓扑优化和其他基线方法。在结构优化任务中，NOTES发现的设计方案实现了低至246的柔度值。实验证明了该方法在降维效率和设计质量方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: NOTES框架成功地将神经算子的表征学习能力与进化策略的鲁棒优化能力相结合，通过在低维潜在空间中嵌入拓扑感知先验，实现了对未见操作条件的高性能设计发现。该方法为偏微分方程控制的物理系统逆设计提供了一个灵活、可迁移的通用框架，有效解决了现有方法在高维空间优化上的局限性。

Abstract: The inverse design of physical systems governed by partial differential equations is computationally demanding due to the high dimensionality and non-convexity of design spaces. Generative models for inverse design often lack robustness and transferability, whereas evolutionary strategies are robust but struggle in high-dimensional spaces. This paper introduces a Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy (NOTES) that integrates dimensionality reduction, representation learning, and evolutionary optimization for efficient and transferable inverse design. NOTES couples a DeepONet-based neural operator with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) to perform global optimization in a compact latent space that encodes topology-aware priors while discovering high-performance designs for unseen operating conditions. Applied to nanophotonic beam-deflector inverse design governed by Maxwell's equations, NOTES reduces the design dimensionality from 256 to 25 and consistently achieves over 95 percent efficiency, outperforming CMA-ES, topology optimization, and other baselines. Applied to structural optimization, NOTES discovers designs that achieve compliance down to 246. By decoupling topology learning of a DeepONet from the governing physics in a PDE solver, NOTES provides a flexible and transferable framework for the inverse design of physical systems.

</details>


### [108] [ECGLight: Compute-Light Framework For Paper ECG Digitization and Myocardial Infarction Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07683)
*Shreyasvi Natraj,Cyrus Achtari,Felice Gragnano,Andrea Milzi,Marco Valgimigli,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的端到端移动端框架，可将纸质心电图的智能手机照片转换为校准的12导联数字信号，并准确筛查心肌梗死病理，为资源受限地区提供可解释的AI诊断支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远地区诊所因网络和计算资源限制仍使用纸质心电图打印件，导致大量纸质ECG无法被现代AI决策支持系统访问，造成急性冠脉闭塞等疾病被忽视和再灌注治疗延迟。现有工作将数字化和诊断分开处理且依赖高资源AI模型，缺乏轻量级、设备端的高保真数字化到诊断的统一框架。

Method: 提出端到端轻量级设备端数字化-诊断流水线：(1)将纸质ECG的智能手机照片或扫描件转换为校准的12导联数字信号；(2)筛查心肌梗死(MI)病理；(3)集成SHAP方法提供模型可解释性。系统在PTB-XL数据集(21,799份ECG)上训练评估，并在医院采集的ECG-Matrix数据集上进一步验证。

Result: 在仅CPU资源上每份ECG处理时间<30秒。MI检测在PTB-XL数据集上达到95.51%准确率(F1=0.9519)，OMI检测在ECG-Matrix数据集上达到88.89%准确率(F1=0.8862)。系统实现了高保真数字化重建和准确的多临床终点支持。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了传统纸质记录可以在世界任何地方可靠地数字化普及，在数字ECG导出、网络连接或高端计算资源不可用时提供可扩展的决策支持，使偏远地区的纸质ECG能够被AI辅助诊断系统有效利用，改善心血管疾病的及时诊断。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is one of the most widely used tests for diagnosing cardiovascular disease. Yet several remote clinics still utilize paper ECG printouts for their analysis due to limited connectivity and computational capacity. As a result, vast numbers of physical ECGs obtained in remote areas still remain incapable of being accessed by contemporary artificial-intelligence (AI)-based decision support as they require high computational resources or strong high-speed internet connectivity. This causes several cases where conditions like acute coronary occlusion (ACS) is overlooked and reperfusion therapy delayed. Although prior work has tackled digitization and diagnosis separately, and utilized advanced AI models for them, there still remains a lack of a compute-light, on-device framework that reconstructs paper ECGs at high fidelity, while accurately supporting multiple clinically relevant endpoints. We address this need with an end-to-end lightweight on-device digitization-to-diagnosis pipeline that converts a smartphone photo or scan of a paper ECG into a calibrated 12-lead signal and screens for Myocardial Infarction (MI) pathologies, with SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to support interpretability. Trained and evaluated on 21,799 ECGs from the PTB-XL dataset and further validated on hospital-acquired ECG-Matrix dataset, the complete system runs in <30 s per ECG on CPU-only resources, achieving 95.51% accuracy (F1 = 0.9519) for MI detection on PTB-XL and 88.89% accuracy (F1 = 0.8862) for OMI detection on ECG-Matrix. This work showcases that legacy paper records can be reliably democratized in any part of the world, providing a scalable decision support when digital ECG export, connectivity, or high-end compute are unavailable

</details>


### [109] [The Key to Going Linear: Analysis-Driven Transformer Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07706)
*Anna Kuzina,Paul N. Whatmough,Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对因果自注意力机制的线性化方法，通过引入sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由等结构化干预措施，在冻结主干网络的条件下显著降低了计算成本，并在大规模模型上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度严重限制了长上下文Transformer推理的性能。尽管存在多种事后线性化方案，但难以确定哪些组件能够有效保持模型质量，特别是在状态更新设计方面缺乏清晰的理解。

Method: 在严格的冻结主干网络条件下，隔离状态更新设计的影响。通过分析softmax机制中依赖于键的秩-1正交投影，揭示delta风格网络优于纯门控累积的原因。引入三种结构化干预措施：sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由，以减少近似误差。在LLaMA和Qwen模型上扩展该方法（最大32B参数）。

Result: 该方法在MMLU基准测试上优于先前的事后线性化基线方法，在长上下文检索任务上与复杂的自适应缓存框架性能相当，同时显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过识别softmax的关键机制特性并针对性地引入结构化干预措施，可以在保持模型性能的同时实现注意力机制的有效线性化，为长上下文Transformer推理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of causal self-attention severely bottlenecks long-context transformer inference. While numerous post hoc linearization pipelines exist, it is difficult to identify which components preserve model quality. This work isolates the effect of state update design in a strict frozen-backbone regime. We show that softmax relies on key-dependent, rank-1 orthogonal projections, elucidating why delta-style networks outperform purely gated accumulation. We identify a potential source of approximation errors and introduce structural interventions, specifically sink tokens, short convolutions, and fixed-budget cache routing, which reduces the remaining gap. We scale this linearization approach across LLaMA and Qwen models up to 32B parameters, outperforming prior post hoc baselines on MMLU and matching the long-context retrieval of complex adaptive-caching frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [A Large Language Model-Driven Agent-Based Modeling Framework with Multi-Round Communication for Simulating Vaccine Opinion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07387)
*Bo Zhang,Na Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个将大型语言模型（Qwen3-8B）集成到基于主体的建模中的框架，通过疫苗接种意见动态案例研究，探讨了不同认知模块（记忆模块和提示多样性模块）对个体决策和宏观意见动态的影响，验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型已被应用于计算社会科学和基于主体的建模中，但特定的认知模块如何驱动个体决策和宏观层面的意见动态仍不清楚。

Method: 提出了一个将LLM（Qwen3-8B）集成到基于主体建模中的框架，以疫苗接种意见动态为案例研究。框架模拟具有异质性特征和社交网络的主体之间的意见动态，通过启用不同的认知模块（记忆模块和提示多样性模块）来评估不同场景。

Result: 模拟结果表明，不同的认知模块对涌现的意见产生相反的影响。此外，该框架成功复现了现有研究中观察到的社会影响的非线性行为模式。

Conclusion: 该框架能够复现社会影响的非线性行为模式，证明了其有效性，并具有达到基于主体建模三级验证水平的潜力。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been utilized in various applications of computational social science and provide the possibility to integrate such models into agent-based modeling to explore the cognitive processes. However, how specific cognitive modules drive individual decisions and macro-level opinion dynamics remains unclear. Therefore, this study introduces a framework that integrates an LLM (Qwen3-8B) into agent-based modeling to investigate this problem, using vaccination opinion dynamics as a case study. We utilize this framework to simulate opinion dynamics among agents with heterogeneous profiles and social networks, evaluating scenarios by enabling different cognitive modules: a memory module and a prompt diversity module. The simulation results reveal that different cognitive modules have opposite impacts on our emergent opinion. Furthermore, the framework reproduces the non-linear behavior patterns of social influence observed in existing research, demonstrating our framework's validity and potential to reach the level 3 validation of agent-based models.

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Agent Robotic Control with Onboard Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07403)
*Kajetan Rachwał,Maciej Majek,Bartłomiej Boczek,Jakub Matejczyk,Dominik Matejkowski,Adam Dąbrowski,Tim Seyde,Alexander Amini,Maria Ganzha*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体系统（MAS）架构，通过在车载硬件上部署专用智能体，使用紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLMs，3-20B参数）控制自主移动机械臂，在模拟工业仓库环境中完成安全检查、仓库维护、包裹质检等五类任务，验证了完全车载计算方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）和视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在机器人控制领域虽有潜力，但在可解释性、泛化能力和计算需求方面面临重大挑战，且通常依赖外部云计算资源。本研究旨在解决这些局限性，实现不依赖外部计算的自主系统。

Method: 提出多智能体系统（MAS）架构，在车载硬件（AMD Ryzen AI迷你PC）上部署专用智能体；采用3-20B参数的紧凑型VLMs，针对包裹检查任务进行微调；设计新型"Megamind"编排智能体，缓解小型模型在长时规划中的上下文保留问题；通过硬件在环仿真在工业仓库场景中进行验证。

Result: 系统成功完成了安全检查、仓库维护、仓库搜索、包裹质量验证和响应人类请求等五类任务；实验结果表明完全车载MAS架构是可行的、具成本效益的云端替代方案，并具有向真实世界迁移的强大潜力。

Conclusion: 完全车载的多智能体系统架构可作为依赖云部署的可行、高效替代方案，为实际机器人应用提供了新的技术路径，仿真环境已以Apache 2.0许可开源发布。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) and Vision Language Action (VLA) models have shown promise in robotic control. Yet, they face significant challenges regarding explainability, generalization, and compute requirements. This paper presents a Multi-Agent System (MAS) architecture that addresses these limitations by deploying specialized agents on onboard hardware - eliminating dependence on external compute. The system controls a multi-purpose autonomous mobile manipulator in a simulated industrial warehouse, fulfilling five task categories: safety inspection, warehouse maintenance, warehouse search, package quality verification, and responding to human requests. Compact VLMs (3-20B parameters) are used throughout, with fine-tuning applied to improve package inspection accuracy. A novel "Megamind" orchestration agent mitigates context retention issues inherent to long-horizon planning with smaller models. The system was validated in a hardware-in-the-loop simulation using an AMD Ryzen(TM) AI mini PC. Results demonstrate that a fully onboard MAS architecture is a viable, cost-efficient alternative to cloud-dependent deployments, with strong potential for real-world transfer. The simulation environment has been released as open source under the Apache 2.0 licence.

</details>


### [112] [Stability and Convergence of Optimistic Exponential Weights with Asymmetric Step Sizes in Bimatrix Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07517)
*Hédi Hadiji,Sarah Sachs*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究双矩阵博弈中乐观指数权重方法的最后迭代收敛性和均衡稳定性，允许双方学习率不同，并给出了以学习率乘积为条件的全局收敛充分判据和稳定性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设双方采用相同的学习率，而在实际应用中两个玩家的学习率往往不同。本文旨在研究不对称学习率下乐观指数权重方法在双矩阵博弈中的收敛性质，以弥补理论空白并解释实证观察到的现象。

Method: 研究双矩阵双人博弈中乐观指数权重（Optimistic Exponential Weights）方法生成的迭代序列，允许双方采用不同的学习率$η_x$和$η_y$。在零和博弈假设不动点集合有限的条件下，建立以学习率乘积$η_xη_y$为核心的全局最后迭代收敛充分条件；对一般双矩阵博弈，推导以学习率乘积表示的渐近稳定性与不稳定性阈值。

Result: （1）在零和博弈中，在不动点集合有限的假设下，给出了仅约束学习率乘积$η_xη_y$的全局最后迭代收敛充分条件，该条件具有实际意义并能部分解释经验观察到的行为；（2）对于一般双矩阵博弈，给出了以学习率乘积表示的渐近稳定性和不稳定性的近似紧阈值，该结果主要具有理论价值；（3）推导出若干已知结果以及特殊情况下的实用学习率界。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入不对称学习率分析，成功建立了以学习率乘积为核心的收敛性和稳定性判据，推广并统一了已有结果，为理解乐观指数权重方法在博弈学习中的动力学行为提供了新的理论工具和实践指导。

Abstract: We study bimatrix two-player games and investigate the last-iterate convergence and stability of equilibria for the iterates generated by the optimistic exponential weights method. In contrast to prior work, we allow the step sizes $η_x$ and $η_y$ to differ. Our first main result establishes, under the assumption that the set of fixed points is finite, a sufficient condition for global last-iterate convergence in the special case of zero-sum games, which constrains only the product $η_xη_y$ of the step sizes. This condition is practically relevant and partially explains empirically observed behavior. Our second main result provides an almost-tight threshold for asymptotic stability and instability, again in terms of products of the step sizes, for general bimatrix games. This result is primarily of theoretical interest. We derive several known results and practically relevant step size bounds for special cases and illustrate our results by experiments.

</details>


### [113] [Agent Delivery Engineering Predictive Reliability Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07689)
*Dexing Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADE预测可靠性框架（ADE-PRF），用于预测长周期LLM多智能体系统的可靠性风险。通过聚合20个异构信号形成Trust Margin指标，实现了8小时健康轨迹预测，在380,227次预测的生产验证中达到MAE=1.228、方向准确率76.8%的性能表现，并首次检测到"虚假繁荣"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期LLM多智能体系统面临着基础设施监控无法感知的可靠性风险，现有监控手段存在盲区。作者希望从被动降解检测转向主动健康轨迹预测，为生产环境中的LLM智能体提供前瞻性的可靠性预警机制。

Method: 提出ADE-PRF框架，通过五层架构聚合20个异构信号形成Trust Margin（TM）指标（动态范围39.2点）。采用三方法并行预测实现8小时预测：指数方法、卡尔曼滤波等。生产验证涵盖15天连续监控，涉及6个智能体档案的380,227次预测和280,579次验证，辅以7次沙箱对照实验。

Result: 指数方法达到MAE=1.228、方向准确率76.8%、99.65%预测在±10点容差内的性能表现，持续优于卡尔曼滤波。首次检测到"虚假繁荣"现象（表面指标正常但隐藏降解）。ADE插件集成后TM立即与真实状态耦合，20个因素中有16个依赖ADE收集数据。

Conclusion: ADE-PRF为生产环境LLM智能体提供了最早的可靠性量化方法之一，具备前瞻性预警能力。该框架成功揭示了传统监控无法感知的隐蔽降解风险，为LLM多智能体系统的可靠性保障提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM multi-agent systems face reliability risks invisible to infrastructure monitoring. We propose the ADE Predictive Reliability Framework (ADE-PRF), enabling proactive health trajectory prediction from passive degradation detection. ADE-PRF aggregates 20 heterogeneous signals across five layers into a Trust Margin (TM) metric (39.2-point dynamic range). Triple-method parallel prediction enables 8-hour forecasts: the Exponential method achieves MAE=1.228, Direction Accuracy=76.8%, with 99.65% within +/-10-point tolerance. Production validation spans 380,227 predictions and 280,579 validations across six agent profiles over 15 continuous days, plus seven sandbox-controlled experiments. Key findings include detection of "false prosperity" -- degradation concealed by normal surface metrics -- and immediate TM coupling with ground-truth states upon ADE plugin integration, with 16/20 factors relying on ADE-collected data. Exponential consistently outperforms Kalman. ADE-PRF provides among the earliest reliability quantification with forward-looking warnings for production LLM agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [From Agentic to Autogenic Network Management for AI-Native 6G and Beyond: A Standards Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06786)
*Petar Djukic,Sudipta Acharya,Takai Eddine Kennouche,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自生成网络管理参考架构，通过赋予系统自我编程、自我反思、自我定位和自我架构能力，推动6G网络管理从代理AI向自主操作演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TM Forum、3GPP和ETSI等标准组织已将代理AI视为下一代网络管理的基础，但在6G网络规模和复杂性下实现这一愿景，需要管理系统在运行时能够自主生成和演进自动化软件。

Method: 引入自生成网络管理参考架构，扩展代理AI能力，增加自我编程、自我反思、自我定位和自我架构四大核心能力，采用分阶段部署策略，从人工监督的基于大型AI模型的代理逐步过渡到自主运行。

Result: 通过TM Forum自主网络用例中的高优先级运营商场景验证，展示了自生成网络管理如何解决实际运营挑战，并证明了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 自生成网络管理架构为6G网络提供了可行的自主管理路径，论文提出了技术路线图，明确了实现这一愿景所需的关键技术突破。

Abstract: Standards bodies, including TM Forum, 3GPP, and ETSI, are converging on Agentic AI as the foundation for next-generation network management, where Large AI Model (LAM)-based agents autonomously interpret intent, coordinate resources, and adapt operational behaviors at runtime. However, achieving this vision at the scale and complexity of 6G networks requires management systems that can generate and evolve their own automation software during operation. We introduce Autogenic network management, a reference architecture that extends agentic capabilities with self-programming, self reflection, self-orienting, and self-architecting capabilities. The architecture supports practical staged deployment beginning with human-supervised LAM-based agents and progressing toward autonomous operation as confidence builds. We demonstrate the approach through high-priority operator scenarios drawn from TM Forum's autonomous network use cases, showing how autogenic management addresses real operational challenges. We conclude with a research roadmap outlining the technical advances needed to make autogenic network management realistic in future 6G networks.

</details>


### [115] [EvoOMG: An Evolution-Oriented Multi-Agent Guidance Framework for Heterogeneous Legacy-and-MLO Wi-Fi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07045)
*Junjie Wu,Lingjian Zhou,Zerui Shao,Yi Zou,Tianrui Li,Yi Zhang,Ziyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoOMG框架，通过演化导向的多智能体决策方法优化Wi-Fi 7/8 MLO与传统设备混合部署场景的吞吐量，在NS-3仿真中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi 7/8多链路操作(MLO)的逐步部署导致传统非MLO站点与支持MLO的站点长期共存，传统站点遵循单链路争用与传输，而MLO站点可利用多链路获取更丰富的接入机会，这种异构混合部署使得吞吐量优化极具挑战性。现有基于学习的方法通常将此类网络视为同构系统，直接将当前观测映射到完整MAC动作，无法准确表征传统单链路和MLO多链路行为。

Method: 提出EvoOMG演化导向多智能体引导框架，将吞吐量优化重构为标准约束的分阶段多智能体决策问题。每个智能体编码近期的信道、队列、争用和传输历史，首先生成争用引导，然后基于前一接入阶段和特定标准可行性约束生成聚合引导。这种自回归设计遵循"先争用后传输"的Wi-Fi MAC顺序，同时保留传统站点和MLO站点的独特协议行为。

Result: NS-3评估表明，与静态增强分布式信道访问(EDCA)、单步MADDPG和独立学习基线相比，EvoOMG在调度吞吐量、收敛稳定性和MLO链路利用率方面均有提升，在代表性混合标准场景中实现显著性能增益。

Conclusion: EvoOMG框架通过自回归设计和分阶段决策有效解决了异构Wi-Fi网络中的吞吐量优化问题，为传统设备与MLO设备长期共存的混合部署场景提供了有效的解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The gradual deployment of Wi-Fi 7/8 multi-link operation (MLO) will lead to long-term coexistence between legacy non-MLO stations (STAs) and MLO-capable STAs in WLANs. This mixed deployment makes throughput optimization challenging because legacy STAs follow single-link contention and transmission, whereas MLO-capable STAs can exploit multiple links with richer access opportunities. Existing learning-based methods usually treat such networks as homogeneous systems and directly map the current observation to a complete MAC action, which cannot faithfully represent both legacy single-link and MLO multi-link behaviors. To address this issue, we propose EvoOMG, an evolution-oriented multi-agent guidance framework for heterogeneous legacy-and-MLO Wi-Fi networks. EvoOMG reformulates throughput optimization as a standard-constrained staged multi-agent decision problem. Each agent encodes recent channel, queue, contention, and transmission histories, first generates contention guidance, and then produces aggregation guidance conditioned on the preceding access stage and standard-specific feasibility constraints. This autoregressive design follows the Wi-Fi MAC order of ``contention before transmission'' while preserving distinct protocol behaviors of legacy and MLO-capable STAs. NS-3 evaluations show that EvoOMG improves scheduled goodput, convergence stability, and MLO link utilization over static enhanced distributed channel access (EDCA), one-step MADDPG, and independent-learning baselines, achieving substantial performance gains in representative mixed-standard scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [How the Fusion of Onboard Sensors and V2X Data can Improve (or not) the Cooperative Perception of Connected Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07114)
*Amir Mohammadisarab,Miguel Sepulcre,Luca Lusvarghi,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了协同感知的有效性，发现其能提升感知水平和范围，但在融合过程中会产生“幽灵车辆”问题，需解决V2X数据误差、丢包和GNSS不准确性等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的车载传感器在恶劣天气或视线受阻时性能下降。协同感知被视为解决方案，但V2X数据与车载传感器融合的有效性及其潜在误差影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 本研究分析了感知测量误差、V2X数据包丢失以及GNSS定位不准确性这三种因素对协同感知有效性的影响。研究通过融合V2X数据与车载传感器数据，评估了在不同误差条件下的感知性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，相较于仅使用车载传感器，协同感知能够显著提升感知水平和感知范围。然而，研究也发现在数据融合过程中会产生“幽灵车辆”（ghost vehicles）这一关键挑战。V2X数据中的误差会导致融合后数据质量下降，影响协同感知的实际效果。

Conclusion: 协同感知具有增强自动驾驶车辆感知能力的潜力，但要充分发挥其优势，必须解决数据融合过程中“幽灵车辆”的生成问题，防止V2X数据在融合时引入额外错误，确保融合数据的可靠性。

Abstract: Automated vehicles rely on onboard sensors to perceive their surroundings and navigate autonomously. However, sensor performance may degrade under adverse weather conditions or when line-of-sight is obstructed. Cooperative perception (or collective perception) is expected to mitigate these limitations by enabling Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) to share sensor data and collaboratively enhance situational awareness. Several studies have analyzed the potential of cooperative perception, yet the fusion of V2X data with information from onboard sensors has received limited focus. V2X data may contain errors that affect the quality of the fused data, and hence the effectiveness of cooperative perception. This study analyzes the impact of sensing measurement errors, V2X packet losses, and GNSS inaccuracies on the effectiveness of cooperative perception. The results highlight the potential of cooperative perception to enhance perception levels and range compared to using onboard sensors alone. However, they also identify key challenges related to the generation of ghost vehicles during the fusion process, which must be addressed to prevent V2X data from introducing additional errors when fused with onboard sensor data.

</details>


### [117] [Unveiling TCP BBR Dominance in Starlink Internet: Experimental Insights and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07133)
*Rakshitha De Silva,Shiva Raj Pokhrel,Jonathan Kua*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究在全球六大洲六个城市部署测试平台，对Google BBR-v3拥塞控制算法在SpaceX Starlink网络上的性能进行了系统性评估。通过与8种主流拥塞控制算法对比，发现BBR-v3在低地球轨道卫星网络中实现了吞吐量、公平性和延迟之间的良好平衡，为下一代卫星互联网传输设计提供了分析基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着SpaceX Starlink等低地球轨道(LEO)卫星网络的快速发展，传统拥塞控制算法在高延迟、动态变化的卫星网络环境中面临挑战。缺乏对最新BBR-v3算法在真实LEO卫星网络中的系统性评估，亟需深入了解其在复杂卫星网络动态下的行为特征和性能表现。

Method: 在全球五大洲六个城市战略部署测试平台，系统性地将BBR-v3与Cubic、Hybla、Vegas、LeoCC、Copa、PCC、BBR-v1和BBR-v2等8种拥塞控制算法进行基准测试。在专用和并发条件下进行实验，开发实用性数学模型来刻画Starlink的复杂网络动态特征，并对BBR-v3行为进行建模分析。通过队列堆积和公平性的广泛评估来验证算法性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，BBR-v3的优势不在于激进的带宽抢占，而是在Starlink互联网上实现了更平衡的公平性、丢包和延迟权衡。BBR-v3能够最大化高延迟、可变卫星环境中的吞吐量，同时在激进性和公平性之间保持平衡。所建立的数学模型成功刻画了Starlink的网络动态特性，为理解实验观察结果提供了分析框架。

Conclusion: BBR-v3被证实是低地球轨道卫星网络中极具竞争力的拥塞控制算法，在Starlink网络环境下展现出优越的性能表现。本研究为下一代卫星互联网传输设计建立了原则性的分析基础，证明了BBR-v3在平衡吞吐量、延迟和公平性方面的有效性，为LEO卫星网络的传输协议优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: This experimental study delivers a global assessment of Google's Bottleneck Bandwidth and Round-trip propagation time-version 3 (BBR-v3) Congestion Control Algorithm (CCA) over SpaceX's Starlink network. Leveraging a strategically deployed six-city testbed across five continents, we systematically benchmark BBR-v3 against eight CCAs: Cubic, Hybla, Vegas, LeoCC, Copa, PCC, BBR-v1, and BBR-v2 under both dedicated and concurrent conditions. Our results demonstrate that BBR-v3's advantage is not aggressive bandwidth capture, but a more balanced fairness, loss, and delay trade-off over the Starlink Internet. We develop pragmatic mathematical models that capture Starlink's complex network dynamics and characterize BBR-v3 behavior to better explain the experimental observations. Our extensive evaluation of queue buildup and fairness further demonstrates BBR-v3's capability to maximize throughput in high-latency, variable satellite environments, while maintaining a balance between aggressiveness and fairness. The findings establish BBR-v3 as a compelling CCA for Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks and provide a principled analytical foundation for next generation satellite Internet transport design.

</details>


### [118] [Small Language Model-based Control for BBR over Low Earth Orbit Satellite Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07142)
*Rakshitha De Silva,Shiva Raj Pokhrel,Jonathan Kua*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了BBR在LEO卫星网络中存在吞吐量与稳定性权衡问题，并提出轻量级语言模型引导的BBR适配框架，在保持高吞吐量的同时显著降低重传开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星互联网带来了快速路径变化、间歇性容量变化和非地面延迟动态等挑战，影响传输层拥塞控制。虽然BBR在此类环境中实现了高吞吐量，但其激进的带宽探测会导致过多重传和LEO链路上的不稳定pacing，存在关键的吞吐量-稳定性权衡问题。

Method: 提出小语言模型(SLM)引导的BBR适配框架，包括：1) 结构化BBR状态编码器；2) 基于LoRA的参数高效微调；3) 受约束的网络头部生成可行的pacing动作。该框架从真实Starlink轨迹中学习相位安全的pacing增益决策，具有低推理延迟。评估使用了GPT-2、T5、GPT-Neo和SmolLM2等模型。

Result: 在跨越6个地理分布式AWS端点的SpaceX Starlink测试平台上的实验表明：BBR始终提供优越的吞吐量，但产生显著更高的重传开销。轻量级SLM能够保持BBR的吞吐量优势，同时大幅减少重传，性能可与大语言模型相媲美，但计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 轻量级语言模型可以有效解决BBR在LEO卫星网络中的稳定性问题，实现高吞吐量与低重传的良好平衡，为非地面网络的传输层优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite Internet introduces rapid path variability, intermittent capacity shifts, and non-terrestrial delay dynamics that challenge transport-layer congestion control. Although Bottleneck Bandwidth and Round-trip propagation time (BBR) achieves high throughput in such environments, its aggressive bandwidth probing can cause excessive retransmissions and unstable pacing over LEO links. This paper presents a global experimental evaluation of BBR over a SpaceX Starlink testbed spanning six geographically distributed AWS endpoints and compares its behaviour against Cubic, Vegas, and Hybla under isolated and competing traffic scenarios. The measurements show that BBR consistently delivers superior throughput but incurs significantly higher retransmission overhead, revealing a critical throughput-stability trade-off in LEO satellite Internet. To address this limitation, we propose a Small Language Model (SLM)-guided BBR adaptation framework that learns phase-safe pacing-gain decisions from real Starlink traces. The framework combines a structured BBR state encoder, LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and a constrained networking head to generate feasible pacing actions with low inference latency. Evaluation using GPT-2, T5, GPT-Neo, and SmolLM2 shows that lightweight SLMs can retain BBR's throughput advantage while substantially reducing retransmissions, with performance comparable to larger language models but at much lower computational cost.

</details>


### [119] [PHaul: A PPO-based forwarding agent for Sub6 enhanced Integrated Access and Backhaul networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07584)
*Jorge Pueyo,Daniel Camps-Mur and,Miguel Catalan-Cid*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Sub6增强型IAB网络架构，并设计了基于深度强化学习的转发引擎PHaul，实现了毫米波与Sub6频段协同优化，在吞吐量和公平性方面分别提升36%和20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3GPP IAB网络通过复用频谱实现毫米波接入网络的经济部署，但存在两大挑战：(1)无线回程段成为性能瓶颈，需要高效的转发策略；(2)毫米波回程性能依赖于视距(LoS)条件，限制了部署灵活性。为解决这些问题，需要通过补充Sub6回程链路来增强网络容量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Sub6增强型IAB网络架构，设计PHaul转发引擎，该引擎结合离线路径选择启发式算法与基于PPO的在线深度强化学习智能体。PHaul利用网络数字孪生技术周期性采样回程网络输入流量并更新流到路径的映射，在真实回程拓扑中的执行时间低于10秒。

Result: 在广泛的网络配置下，PHaul相比两种替代启发式算法，吞吐效率提升高达36%，公平性提升高达20%。同时证明PHaul对训练和推理阶段网络拓扑差异具有鲁棒性，能够适应实际中因链路故障导致的拓扑变化。

Conclusion: PHaul转发引擎能够有效解决IAB网络回程瓶颈和LoS依赖问题，通过混合频段协同优化显著提升网络性能，具有良好的实用性和鲁棒性，为毫米波IAB网络部署提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: 3GPP Integrated Access and Backhaul (IAB) allows operators to deploy outdoor mm-wave access networks in a cost-efficient manner, by reusing the same spectrum in access and backhaul. In IAB networks the performance bottleneck is the wireless backhaul segment, where efficient forwarding strategies are needed to effectively use the available capacity. In addition, the performance of the mm-wave IAB backhaul segment is contingent on the availability of line of sight (LoS) conditions in the selected deployment sites. To mitigate LoS dependence, in this paper, we propose to complement the mm-wave backhaul segment of IAB networks with additional Sub6 backhaul links, which contribute to the capacity and robustness of the backhaul network. We refer to IAB networks combining Sub6 and mm-wave links in the backhaul as Sub6 enhanced IAB networks. In this context, the main contribution of this paper is PHaul, a forwarding engine for Sub6 enhanced IAB networks that accomodates different traffic engineering criteria, and combines an offline path selection heuristic with an online Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agent based on Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). By leveraging a network digital twin of the IAB wireless backhaul, PHaul periodically samples the input traffic of the backhaul network and updates flow to path mappings, with execution times below 10 seconds in realistic backhaul topologies. We present an exhaustive performance evaluation, where we demonstrate that PHaul can achieve gains of up to 36\% in throughput efficiency and of up to 20\% in fairness, when compared against two alternative heuristics in a wide range of network configurations. We also demonstrate that PHaul is robust to differences between the network topologies considered in the training and inference phases, which can occur in practice due to link failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Seekable OCI: Lazy-Loading Container Images via Range-Request Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06868)
*James Thompson,Wayne Mesard,Jesse Butler,Sri Saran Balaji Rajakumar,Henry Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SOCI通过懒加载架构实现容器镜像按需拉取，将冷启动拉取时间从20秒降至2.8秒（7.4倍加速），且拉取时间与镜像大小无关，已在Amazon EKS和ECS Fargate生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器镜像拉取占Kubernetes环境中Pod启动时间的主要部分。标准拉取方式在容器启动前需下载整个镜像，即使应用启动时仅访问镜像内容的一小部分，导致启动延迟过长。

Method: 提出SOCI（Seekable OCI）懒加载架构：1）为标准OCI镜像构建外部索引，将文件映射到压缩层内的字节范围；2）索引存储为OCI referrer artifact，无需修改镜像、注册表或部署工具；3）运行时通过FUSE文件系统拦截文件访问，使用HTTP范围请求按需获取数据；4）支持懒加载和并行全量拉取两种模式。

Result: 在1.3GB Python Web服务镜像上，冷启动拉取时间从20秒降至约2.8秒（7.4倍加速）；2.5GB镜像获得9.3倍加速。拉取时间独立于镜像大小（常数时间），而标准拉取呈线性增长。在80%访问密度处存在交叉点：低于此值懒加载更快，高于此值并行全量拉取更快。

Conclusion: SOCI无需修改镜像格式和标准注册表即可实现容器懒加载启动，已在Amazon EKS和ECS Fargate生产环境部署（Prime Day 2025期间每日启动1840万任务），自2023年起稳定提供懒加载服务，EKS Auto Mode在GPU实例上使用SOCI并行拉取模式。

Abstract: Container image pulling accounts for the majority of pod startup time in Kubernetes environments. Standard pull downloads the entire image before the container can start, even when the application accesses only a fraction of the image content at startup. We present SOCI (Seekable OCI), a lazy-loading architecture that enables containers to start without downloading the full image. SOCI builds an external index over standard OCI images, mapping files to byte ranges within compressed layers. At runtime, a FUSE filesystem intercepts file accesses and serves them via HTTP range requests. Unlike prior approaches that require image format conversion, SOCI works with unmodified images and standard registries. The index is stored as an OCI referrer artifact, requiring no changes to images, registries, or deployment tooling. On a 1.3 GB Python web service image, SOCI reduces cold-start pull time from 20 seconds to approximately 2.8 seconds (7.4x speedup), with pull time independent of image size. Larger images see larger speedups (9.3x on a 2.5 GB image) because SOCI pull time is constant while standard pull scales linearly. We measure a crossover at 80% access density: below this, lazy loading wins; above, parallel full pull is faster. SOCI lazy loading is deployed in production on Amazon EKS and Amazon ECS Fargate (which launched 18.4 million tasks per day during Prime Day 2025), and has been serving lazy-load requests since 2023. EKS Auto Mode uses SOCI's parallel pull mode for GPU instances.

</details>


### [121] [Voltron: Enabling Elastic Multi-Device Execution of LLM Inference for Empowered Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07046)
*Chanwoo Cho,Wooseok Kim,Yonglak Son,Young Seo Lee,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Voltron框架，利用多个边缘设备协同执行大语言模型推理，相比单设备方案准确率提升高达16.5%，同时满足用户QoS需求并保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式LLM推理服务存在响应延迟高、通信开销大和隐私泄露风险等问题，而单一边缘设备的资源限制制约了可执行的模型规模和准确率。

Method: 提出Voltron框架，弹性利用多个用户端设备协同执行LLM推理，通过分布式部署使更大的模型能够在边缘环境中运行，并自适应多样化的现实边缘环境。

Result: 实验表明，Voltron相比可在单一边缘设备上执行的最先进LLM方案，准确率提升高达16.5%，同时满足用户的QoS需求。

Conclusion: 利用多设备协同推理是突破单一边缘设备资源限制的有效途径，能够在保护隐私和降低延迟的同时显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in intelligent services due to their remarkable capability in generative tasks. Typically, LLM-based services process the inference requests of the users in a centralized data center. Unfortunately, such centralized execution has limitations for end-users, such as increased response latency with communication overhead and privacy leakage risk. To alleviate the aforementioned limitations, there have been increasing pushes to execute LLM inference locally on user-end devices. However, the limited resources of a single edge device impose restrictions on achievable accuracy of LLMs. To overcome the issue, we first propose to leverage multiple user-end devices available at the edge for LLM inference, enabling the execution of larger models. Specifically, we propose Voltron, a novel on-device LLM inference framework that elastically utilizes multiple user-end devices for LLM inference execution while adapting to diverse real-world edge environments. In our evaluation, Voltron achieves up to 16.5% higher accuracy than state-of-the-art LLMs that can be executed on a single edge device, satisfying user QoS requirements.

</details>


### [122] [GIFT: Geometry-Informed Low-precision Gradient Communication for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07494)
*Jieying Wang,Shuyuan Fan,Mingkai Zheng,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出GIFT方法，通过几何感知坐标变换实现低精度梯度通信，在Llama-600M预训练中减少7.6%时间并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练中梯度通信是主要扩展瓶颈。现有低精度量化方法（如FP8、NVFP4）在欧几里得空间进行线性或非线性映射，由于梯度高度各向异性导致方向相关失真，严重损害模型性能。

Method: GIFT在量化前将梯度变换到近似各向同性空间，使低精度表示更忠实于高精度对应项。该方法仅改变低精度梯度通信的坐标系，不改变优化器、训练配方、通信集合或低精度格式。通过低秩近似和选择性应用来平衡计算开销与通信减少。

Result: 在Llama-300M和Llama-600M模型上的实验表明：GIFT在64个NVIDIA GH200超级芯片上将Llama-600M的端到端预训练时间减少7.6%，同时相比直接欧几里得FP8通信，在相同优化器和通信路径下改善了下游任务保持性能。

Conclusion: 几何感知的坐标变换可以有效解决各向异性梯度在低精度量化中的失真问题，GIFT实现了通信效率与模型性能的双重优化，为大语言模型分布式训练提供了有效的梯度通信解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient communication is a primary scaling bottleneck in large language model (LLM) pretraining. Communicating gradients in low-precision formats, such as FP8 and NVFP4, can significantly reduce the communication volume. Existing methods quantize gradients via linear or nonlinear mappings in Euclidean space, often degrading model performance because highly anisotropic gradients incur direction-dependent distortion. We present GIFT, a geometry-informed gradient scaling method that performs low-precision communication in geometry-aware coordinates. By transforming gradients into a near-isotropic space before quantization, GIFT makes low-precision representations substantially more faithful to their high-precision counterparts. GIFT only changes the coordinate system used for low-precision gradient communication and does not change the optimizer, training recipe, communication collective, or low-precision format. We also develop a simplified geometry-aware transformation algorithm with low-rank approximation and selective application to balance the computation overhead and communication reduction. We examine the empirical convergence of GIFT using Llama-300M and Llama-600M models. Our results show that GIFT reduces the end-to-end pretraining time of Llama-600M by 7.6% on 64 NVIDIA GH200 Superchips, while improving the downstream task preservation profile over direct Euclidean FP8 communication under the same optimizer and communication path.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [HiFuzz: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Semantic-Aware and Adaptive CPU Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06619)
*Ya Wang,Hanwei Fan,Zhenguo Liu,Xiaofeng Zhou,Yangdi Lyu,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HiFuzz，一种基于分层强化学习的处理器验证框架，通过双层生成过程和自适应奖励机制，在RISC-V核上显著提升了覆盖率和漏洞检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代处理器验证中，传统的基于变异的模糊测试在到达深层架构状态方面效率低下，难以有效覆盖复杂的处理器状态空间。

Method: 提出HiFuzz框架，采用分层强化学习方法，包含两层生成过程：Program Agent负责全局布局，Basic Block Agent负责精确指令填充。为解决奖励稀疏问题，引入自适应覆盖率奖励机制和语义感知的基本块编码器提供内在反馈。

Result: 在三个真实的RISC-V处理器核上进行广泛评估，结果表明HiFuzz在覆盖率和漏洞检测方面显著优于当前最先进的模糊测试工具。

Conclusion: HiFuzz通过结构化的分层生成过程和创新的自适应奖励机制，有效解决了传统变异式模糊测试的局限性，为处理器验证领域提供了一种更高效的方法。

Abstract: Modern processor verification struggles to reach deep architectural states due to the inefficiencies of traditional mutation-based fuzzing. We propose HiFuzz, a novel hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that replaces mutation with a structured, two-layer generation process: a Program Agent for global layout and a Basic Block Agent for precise instruction filling. To overcome reward sparsity, HiFuzz integrates an adaptive coverage reward mechanism and a semantic-aware basic block encoder providing intrinsic feedback. Extensive evaluations on three real-world RISC-V cores demonstrate that HiFuzz significantly outperforms state-of-the-art fuzzers in coverage and bug detection.

</details>


### [124] [ThermoDSE: A Thermal-Aware and Comprehensive Design Space Exploration for Chiplet-Based DNN Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07096)
*Jian Peng,Hanwei Fan,Jingbo Jiang,Lin Jiang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了ThermoDSE，这是首个针对Chiplet架构DNN加速器的热感知设计空间探索框架，实现了3.5倍的能效-延迟-良率改进和显著的运行时加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chiplet架构DNN加速器在平衡性能和良率方面具有可扩展性优势，但面临关键的面积和热约束挑战。现有设计空间优化方法缺乏对细粒度任务建模、chiplet粒度、核心粒度和关键物理约束的联合考虑。

Method: 提出ThermoDSE框架，将现有细粒度建模技术集成到统一的仿真和优化框架中，在严格的热和面积约束下联合考虑架构设计、任务编排和chiplet间通信。

Result: 相比最先进的Simba和其他基线，在能效-延迟-良率指标上实现最高3.5倍改进；相比模拟退火和强化学习方法，分别实现3.7倍和29.4倍的运行时加速，并收敛到更好的设计点。

Conclusion: ThermoDSE成功解决了chiplet架构DNN加速器设计中的多维度优化挑战，证明了热感知和综合设计空间探索的重要性和有效性。

Abstract: Chiplet-based DNN accelerators provide a scalable path to balance performance and yield for modern AI workloads. However, such systems face critical challenges in area and thermal constraints. Design space optimization should jointly consider fine-grained task modeling, chiplet granularity, core granularity, and critical physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework that involves all these factors.
  In this work, we propose ThermoDSE, a thermal-aware and comprehensive design space exploration framework for chiplet-based DNN accelerators. ThermoDSE integrates existing fine-grained modeling techniques into a unified simulation and optimization framework that jointly considers architecture design, task orchestration, and inter-chiplet communication under strict thermal and area constraints. Experimental results show that ThermoDSE achieves up to 3.5x improvement in Energy-Delay-Inverse-Yield, defined as E times D times inverse Y, compared with state-of-the-art Simba and other baselines. Furthermore, relative to simulated annealing and reinforcement learning-based methods, ThermoDSE converges to better design points with 3.7x and 29.4x runtime speedups, respectively.

</details>


### [125] [Miter-Aware LUT Mapping: Aligning Structure and Solvability for Efficient Logic Equivalence Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07164)
*Jiaying Zhu,Zhengyuan Shi,Mengxia Tao,Kezhi Li,Min Li,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LUT的miter感知映射框架，通过保留电路间的结构对应关系并显式表达高层逻辑关系，在SAT求解器上实现了高达92.1%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑等价性检查（LEC）是硬件验证的基础任务，但常受综合引起的结构扰动和XOR密集区域影响，导致SAT求解器性能下降。作者认为miter的建模方式与SAT求解器本身同样关键，因此需要一种求解器感知的建模范式来提升LEC效率。

Method: 提出了一种miter感知映射框架，集成三种技术：（1）等价保持映射：从结构上对齐两个电路；（2）高斯引导的XOR建模：代数简化密集算术运算；（3）面向求解器的LUT选择：生成优化的SAT推理表示。该方法构建基于LUT的miter而非传统的扁平网表，保留了关键的结构对应关系。

Result: 在综合数据集上的评估表明，该方法在最先进的SAT求解器上实现了高达92.1%的性能提升（推测为运行时间减少），证明了求解器感知建模范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 将结构映射与SAT推理相统一的求解器感知建模范式能够从根本上提升LEC效率，miter的建模方式对LEC性能具有重要影响。

Abstract: Logic Equivalence Checking (LEC), a fundamental hardware verification task, is often bottlenecked by synthesis-induced structural perturbations and XOR-dense regions that degrade SAT solver performance. We contend that the modeling of the miter is as critical as the SAT solver itself. To this end, we introduce a miter-aware mapping framework that strategically formulates the problem before solving. By constructing a LUT-based miter -- instead of a traditional, flat netlist -- our approach preserves critical structural correspondence between the two designs while making high-level logic relations explicit. Our framework uniquely integrates three techniques: equivalence-preserving mapping to structurally align the two circuits, Gaussian-guided XOR modeling to algebraically simplify dense arithmetic, and solver-oriented LUT selection to generate a representation optimized for efficient SAT reasoning. Evaluated on comprehensive datasets, our method achieves up to a \textbf{92.1\%} reduction across state-of-the-art SAT solvers. This demonstrates that a solver-aware modeling paradigm, which unifies structural mapping with SAT reasoning, can fundamentally enhance LEC efficiency.

</details>


### [126] [Vectorizing Quantum Control: A RISC-V Vector Extension Architecture for Scalable Qubit Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07372)
*Xiaorang Guo,Kun Qin,Yanbin Chen,Carsten Trinitis,Martin Schulz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RISC-V向量扩展的量子控制处理器设计，通过向量化和硬件协议优化实现了对多达128个量子比特的高效控制，相比基线方案加速达2.52倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子控制处理器设计主要依赖定制指令集，限制了设计复用性，构建支持工具链需要大量工作。同时，在高扩展性场景下高效寻址量子比特和调度操作仍然是关键挑战。

Method: 构建基于RISC-V向量（RVV）引擎的量子控制方法，配备面向量子的扩展，利用RVV的高并行性单指令寻址多达128个量子比特；将参数化旋转信息嵌入指令集，支持混合量子-经典程序中门旋转的动态调节；设计基于硬件的暂停-恢复协议以支持线路中间测量。

Result: 使用RISC-V工具链和FPGA原型进行全面评估，设计在程序执行时间上相比基线实现高达2.52倍的加速，具有优异的扩展性；硬件暂停-恢复协议在接收到测量结果后80纳秒内恢复流水线执行。

Conclusion: 基于RISC-V向量扩展的量子控制处理器设计有效解决了现有方案的复用性和扩展性问题，通过向量化和硬件协议优化显著提升了程序执行性能，为量子控制处理器设计提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The Quantum Control Processor (QCP) bridges the gap between compiler toolchains and control electronics, and is responsible for translating compiled quantum circuits into executable instructions that directly manipulate qubits and handle measurement feedback. However, existing designs rely primarily on customized instruction sets, limiting design reuse and requiring significant effort to build supporting toolchains. Furthermore, efficiently addressing qubits and scheduling operations in highly scalable scenarios remains a critical challenge. In this work, we present a vectorized quantum control approach built upon the RISC-V Vector (RVV) engine with a quantum-oriented extension. Leveraging the high parallelism of RVV, our approach can address up to 128 qubits in a single instruction. We also embed parameterized rotation information into the instruction set, enabling dynamic tuning of gate rotations in hybrid quantum-classical programs. To support mid-circuit measurements, we design a hardware-based halt-resume protocol that resumes pipeline execution within 80 $ns$ of receiving the measurement result. Comprehensive evaluation using both RISC-V toolchains and FPGA prototypes demonstrates that our design achieves up to 2.52$\times$ speedup over the baseline in program execution time, with excellent scalability.

</details>


### [127] [ATLAS: Automated HLS for DL-Optimized FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07643)
*Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS自动化流程，实现从高层DL模型描述到含定制硬核的FPGA实现的自动映射，无需手动RTL设计，通过GEMM抽象层解耦前端编译与后端硬件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA架构集成领域特定硬核加速DL推理，但需手动RTL设计和显式实例化；HLS工具不支持原生硬核代码生成，黑盒机制仍需大量重复工作；DL领域亟需比C/C++更高层的编程接口（如Pytorch/Keras）以提升可编程性和用户采纳度。

Method: 采用GEMM作为通用抽象层，包含两个组件：(1) hls4ml-GEMM编译器前端，将DL层转换为含架构无关GEMM函数调用的HLS C代码；(2) GEMM IP生成器后端，生成基于硬核的RTL封装，包含分块逻辑、控制FSM和调度元数据。

Result: 在11个DL设计上进行评估，包括全连接层、卷积层、注意力层，以及完整的CNN、MLP和Transformer模型，目标FPGA架构采用Tensor Slices，使用Catapult进行HLS和VTR进行实现验证。

Conclusion: ATLAS实现了从高层DL模型到含定制硬核FPGA实现的完全自动化流程，消除了端用户的手动RTL设计和显式硬核实例化需求，有效解决了硬核编程复杂性问题。

Abstract: FPGA architectures increasingly incorporate domain-specific in-fabric hardblocks to accelerate DL inference, particularly GEMM, which dominates DL computation. To realize the performance gains of these hardblocks, manual RTL design is required: the programmer must understand the hardblock microarchitecture, instantiate them in RTL, and manage tiling and control logic. While programming in C/C++ and using HLS tools has increased the abstraction level and productivity of FPGA engineers, HLS tools do not support code generation for custom hardblocks natively. Prior work has demonstrated that blackbox mechanisms in HLS tools can be used to target custom hardblocks, but this still requires explicit function calls in user-written HLS C and manual creation of RTL IP libraries, significant effort that must be repeated for every layer in a DL model. Furthermore, for DL, an even high-level programming interface, e.g., Pytorch/Keras instead of C/C++, is desirable for improved programmability and user adoption.
  We present ATLAS, a fully automated flow from a high-level DL model description to a hardware implementation on an FPGA with custom in-fabric DL-optimized hardblocks, requiring no manual RTL design or explicit hardblock instantiation from the end user. Our approach uses GEMM as a universal abstraction layer and comprises two components: (1) hls4ml-GEMM, a compiler frontend that transforms DL layers into HLS C code with architecture-agnostic GEMM function calls, and (2) a GEMM IP Generator, an architecture-aware backend that produces hardblock-based RTL wrappers with tiling logic, control FSMs, and scheduling metadata. We evaluate the flow across 11 DL designs, including individual fully connected, convolution, and attention layers, as well as full CNN, MLP, and Transformer models targeting an FPGA architecture with Tensor Slices using Catapult for HLS and VTR for implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [AgentLens: Production-Assessed Trajectory Reviews for Coding Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06624)
*Andrey Podivilov,Vadim Lomshakov,Sergey Savin,Matvei Startsev,Roman Pozharskiy,Maksim Parshin,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentLens是一个面向交互式代码智能体的生产级评估基准，超越传统的二元通过/失败判定，通过评估完整执行轨迹来提供可解释的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体基准测试仅关注任务是否通过，忽略了用户实际体验的完整交互轨迹，包括指令遵循、工具使用、自我验证、错误恢复和人机交互等关键维度。

Method: 结合形式化验证（客观检查）、大语言模型编写的轨迹评审和并排比较方法，对智能体完整执行轨迹进行评估，为每次运行生成可读性强的评分解释。

Result: AgentLens不仅可用于模型排名，还能有效诊断模型行为、比较智能体版本迭代，并在夜间评估流程中检测产品退化问题。

Conclusion: AgentLens为代码智能体提供了更全面、可解释的评估框架，已作为开源基准发布，支持生产环境中的持续评估与改进。

Abstract: We present AgentLens, a production-assessed benchmark for interactive code agents. Most code-agent benchmarks reduce a run to a single bit -- did the task pass? -- but the people who actually use these agents experience the entire trajectory: how the agent follows instructions, uses its tools, verifies its own work, recovers from mistakes, and talks to them along the way. AgentLens evaluates that whole trajectory. It pairs formal verification, where an objective check exists, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and side-by-side comparisons, so that each run yields a readable explanation of why the score is what it is. This makes AgentLens useful for more than ranking models: we use it to diagnose model behavior, compare successive versions of our own agent, and catch product regressions in a nightly evaluation pipeline. We release the benchmark as open source at https://github.com/agent-lens/agent-lens-bench.

</details>


### [129] [LLM-powered reasoning in agent-based modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06757)
*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合基于代理和语言驱动的流行病建模框架(HALE)，利用大语言模型预测人类决策，解决传统ABM依赖静态先验知识的问题，并以COVID-19在盐湖县的传播为例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于代理建模(ABM)依赖静态先验知识，无法适应实时变化，在政策制定场景中存在信息缺口。大语言模型为预测人类决策行为提供了新机遇，因此需要开发能够利用LLM能力的新型ABM框架。

Method: 开发了可扩展的混合基于代理和语言驱动的流行病建模框架(HALE)，该框架在ABM仿真中利用大语言模型来预测人类的决策行为，实现了动态适应性建模。

Result: 以犹他州盐湖县的COVID-19疫情及其影响作为概念验证案例，成功应用HALE框架进行了仿真模拟，证明了该方法在流行病建模中的可行性。

Conclusion: HALE框架成功整合了大语言模型与基于代理建模方法，为流行病建模和政策制定提供了新的动态适应性工具，具有可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [130] [When Does In-Context Search Help? A Sampling-Complexity Theory of Reflection-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06720)
*Yotam Wolf,Noam Wies,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型的上下文搜索进行理论分析，将其建模为推理轨迹的近似推断，证明了当自我反思能准确定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可实现指数级改进，且这种优势具有鲁棒性和可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型中上下文搜索的理论基础，即模型通过迭代生成、批判和修正解决方案尝试来实现在线搜索的机制，并分析其在推理时的采样复杂度。

Method: 将上下文搜索建模为推理轨迹上的近似推断过程，其中基础模型定义先验分布，自我反思提供后验更新的反馈信号。采用分阶段强化学习抽象框架，分析最优策略扩展如何实现后验重加权。

Result: 证明了两个核心结论：（1）当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可达到指数级改进，仅用多项式数量的顺序尝试即可解决零样本通过率指数级小的问题；（2）当该性质失效时，基于历史尝试的条件化相比并行采样无渐近优势。此外，近似后验更新已足够，且在搜索轨迹上的交叉熵训练能以多项式样本复杂度恢复所需行为。

Conclusion: 在可验证奖励的分阶段强化学习抽象下，最优策略扩展实现相同的后验重加权规则，验证了理论在大规模推理模型上的关键定性预测。这为理解和改进LLM的推理能力提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with extended reasoning has enabled in-context search, in which models iteratively generate, critique, and revise solution attempts. We provide a theoretical analysis of in-context search by modeling it as approximate inference over reasoning traces, where the base model defines a prior and self-reflection provides feedback for posterior updates, and study the resulting inference-time sampling complexity - the number of sequential attempts needed to achieve high success probability. We show that when reflections reliably localize early mistakes, in-context search can yield exponential improvements over the base model, solving problems with exponentially small zero-shot pass rates using only a polynomial number of sequential attempts, whereas when this property fails, conditioning on past attempts offers no asymptotic benefit over parallel sampling. We further show that these gains are robust and learnable: approximate posterior updates suffice, and cross-entropy training on search rollouts recovers the required behavior with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, we show that under a stagewise abstraction of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the optimal policy extension implements the same posterior reweighting rule. We validate key qualitative predictions of the theory on real large reasoning models.

</details>


### [131] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统的安全性评估常将直接提示与规划器-执行器流程的差异合并为单一“流程效应”，本文提出五条件对照设计，分离出有害意图重构、规划器行为和委托框架三种机制，发现聚合的流程安全性并非稳定的架构属性，意图重构是最主要的风险信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将多智能体LLM系统的安全性差异简化为单一的“流程效应”，这种聚合度量难以解释，因为它混淆了三种机制：有害意图被重构为看似合理的操作任务、规划器可能拒绝或转换请求、执行器在暗示已获批准的委托提示下执行。需要分离这些因素以准确理解多智能体系统的安全风险来源。

Method: 提出五条件对照实验设计，在30个合成有害场景和来自四个智能体安全基准的外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM判断合规性。通过分离操作重构、规划器行为和委托框架三个机制，系统分析各因素对安全性的独立影响。

Result: 聚合流程安全性不是稳定的架构属性。操作重构是最主要的风险信号，在GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek上均增加合规性，Claude相对抵抗。规划器主要通过拒绝来缓解风险，但当规划器产生可执行步骤时，执行器合规性可能超过直接操作基线。委托框架对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感。直接提示排名可能误判部署后的规划器-执行器行为。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全性评估应分别报告重构效应、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对，而非将失败归因于架构本身。聚合度量会掩盖重要的交互效应，独立的机制分析对于理解和改进多智能体LLM系统安全性至关重要。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [132] [From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321)
*Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSOP框架，通过将原子操作合成为可复用的标准操作流程(SOPs)来实现智能体的自我进化，显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体框架主要依赖由细粒度原子操作（如基本文件I/O或单轮搜索）组成的静态工具集，这迫使智能体为每个重复工作流重新发明底层逻辑，导致推理开销增加和失败率上升。

Method: 提出EvoSOP框架，使智能体能够从执行轨迹中提取标准操作流程(SOPs)，并将其封装为可调用的高阶工具；通过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的系统生命周期迭代优化工具集。

Result: 实验结果表明，EvoSOP显著提升了任务成功率，同时相比基线方法大幅减少了交互轮次；迭代工具优化促进了可靠且高效的工具使用模式。

Conclusion: 该研究为自进化智能体的发展提供了一条可扩展的路径，证明了通过SOP合成和工具集迭代优化可以有效提升智能体的性能和效率。

Abstract: Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [133] [QANTIS: Hardware-Calibrated Sequential POMDP Belief Updates on IBM Heron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06760)
*Bayram Yuksel Eker,Suayb S. Arslan,Ozgur Nazli,Mustafa Serhat Demirgil,Furkan Deligoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在IBM Heron硬件上研究了QANTIS量子信念更新服务在Tiger POMDP序列决策中的可重用性，发现全步骤固定点振幅放大能够在硬件上保持后验分布的一致性，为量子信念更新原语建立了操作边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中的自主系统基于信念而非原始传感器数据进行决策。QANTIS将量子处理器视为信念更新服务，提供先验和观测模型后估计稀有事件证据项并返回后验给经典规划器。本文旨在验证该量子信念更新服务能否在IBM Heron硬件上跨时序重用而不破坏规划器所需的 posterior 分布质量。

Method: 采用受控硬件案例研究，在Tiger POMDP上比较三种振幅放大策略：无放大、受保护Grover放大、全步骤固定点振幅放大(FPAA)。在相同轨迹上运行8步、12步主要实验及20步、32步对照实验，检验返回的后验是否改变下游动作选择。引入边界感知BIQAE稳定接近零和一的振幅估计，并通过稀有事件扫描映射百万分之一证据的逻辑采样复杂度边界。

Result: 全步骤FPAA在8步和12步主要运行中保持了Tiger后验分布，20步和32步对照组仍在相同操作频带内。所有报告的决策检查中，硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择了相同的即时动作。边界感知BIQAE成功稳定了边界区域的振幅估计，稀有事件扫描确定了百万分之一证据的采样复杂度包络。

Conclusion: 研究确立了硬件校准信念更新原语的操作边界，证明了量子信念更新服务在当前IBM Heron硬件上的可行性。这是一个硬件校准原语的操作边界研究，而非端到端自主性或时钟加速优势声明，为量子增强POMDP求解提供了基础构件。

Abstract: Autonomous systems under partial observability act on beliefs, not raw sensor events. QANTIS treats the quantum processor as a calibrated belief-update service in that loop: it receives a prior and an observation model, estimates the rare-event evidence term, and returns an ordinary posterior to a classical planner. This paper asks whether that service can be reused across a sequential Tiger POMDP horizon on present IBM Heron hardware without corrupting the planner-facing posterior. We answer with a controlled hardware case study rather than an end-to-end autonomy or wall-clock speedup claim. The study compares no amplification, guarded Grover amplification, and all-step fixed-point amplification on the same trajectory, then checks whether the returned posterior would change the downstream action. All-step FPAA preserves the Tiger posterior across the reported 8-step and 12-step primary runs, and the 20-step and 32-step controls remain inside the same operating band. In every reported decision check, the hardware posterior and the exact Bayes posterior select the same immediate action. Boundary-aware BIQAE stabilizes amplitude estimation near zero and near one, while a rare-event sweep maps the logical sample-complexity envelope for one-in-a-million evidence. The result is an operating envelope for a hardware-calibrated belief-update primitive, not a standalone hardware-advantage claim.

</details>


### [134] [Cost-Effective Agent Harnesses for Abstract Reasoning and Generalization on ARC-AGI-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06764)
*Kabir Moghe,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种不依赖ARC专项训练或大量测试时计算的架构方法。通过Explorer-Definer Pipeline和Reflective Orchestrator两种代理架构，将模型基线从15.50%提升至67.25% pass@2，成本仅$0.62/任务。分析表明系统受限于生成能力而非选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARC-AGI-1进展主要来自两类方法：前沿模型的大量测试时计算（进化搜索、穷举采样、扩展思维链），或针对基准的专项训练（小模型在ARC数据上微调）。本文探索第三种路径：在严格预算下使用开放权重模型（DeepSeek V3.2）非思考模式，不进行ARC专项微调，仅通过架构设计恢复性能。

Method: 提出两种架构：1) Explorer-Definer Pipeline，将模式发现与可执行变换合成分离为两阶段代理流水线；2) Reflective Orchestrator，当假设在训练对上失败时自主探索新变换，增强流水线的自适应重探索能力。这些架构显式分解模式发现和程序合成阶段。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1公开400任务评估集上：Pipeline达到57.50% pass@2（成本$0.25/任务），Orchestrator达到67.25% pass@2（成本$0.62/任务）。相比15.50%单次基线提升约52个百分点。无偏pass@k分析表明系统受限于生成而非选择（基于训练对准确率的选择捕获约95%候选上限）。Orchestrator通过自适应重探索验证了该预测（无偏pass@1提升+9.81 pp，与选择介导的pass@2提升匹配）。消融实验显示think工具是重要组件，移除后pass@2下降5.75 pp。

Conclusion: 证明了在无ARC专项训练和重测试时计算的情况下，仅通过精心设计的代理架构即可显著提升性能。研究提供了可证伪诊断：系统改进需要更广泛的生成而非更好的排序，Orchestrator通过实现自适应重探索验证了这一预测。为理解并突破模型在抽象推理任务中的瓶颈提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent progress on ARC-AGI-1 from disclosed architectures has come broadly from two regimes: heavy test-time compute over frontier models (evolutionary search, exhaustive sampling, extended chain-of-thought), or benchmark-specific training in which small models are fine-tuned on ARC data, often with task-specialized architectures. We study a third regime: an open-weight model in non-thinking mode (DeepSeek V3.2) under a strict budget, with no ARC-specific fine-tuning. We study what is recoverable through architecture alone, building agentic harnesses that decompose pattern-discovery and program-synthesis stages explicitly. First, we introduce an Explorer-Definer Pipeline that separates pattern discovery from executable transformation synthesis, implemented as a two-stage agent pipeline. Next, we present the Reflective Orchestrator, which augments the pipeline with autonomous exploration of new transformations when previous hypotheses fail on training pairs. On the ARC-AGI-1 public 400-task evaluation set, the pipeline reaches 57.50% pass@2 at \$0.25 per task, and the orchestrator reaches 67.25% pass@2 at \$0.62 per task. Together these architectures lift a 15.50% one-shot baseline by ~52 points without benchmark-specific training or heavy test-time compute. Furthermore, the orchestrator-driven lift tests a falsifiable diagnostic the pipeline produces; unbiased pass@k analysis suggests the pipeline is generation-bound, not selection-bound (selection via training-pair accuracy captures ~95% of the candidate ceiling) and predicts that significant improvement requires broader generation, not better ranking. The orchestrator implements this prediction via adaptive re-exploration and confirms it (unbiased pass@1 lift +9.81 pp, matching selection-mediated pass@2 lift). An additional pipeline ablation identifies its think tool as a significant component, with removal reducing pass@2 by 5.75 pp.

</details>


### [135] [Institutional Red-Teaming: Deployment Rules, Not Just Models, Causally Shape Multi-Agent AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07695)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“制度化红队测试”方法，通过只改变多智能体AI系统中的部署规则来评估其对集体安全的影响。在IABench-CA基准测试中发现：部署规则会显著改变集体安全性；不存在通用安全默认规则，但针对身份的规则普遍具有危险性；身份显著性是导致风险的关键机制。作者提出了安全案例工作流程，以认证每个部署场景的临时安全规则区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体AI系统的部署日益增多，如何评估和确保这些系统的集体安全性成为关键挑战。现有评估方法难以分离单个部署规则对集体行为的影响，因此需要一种系统性的方法来测试部署规则对多智能体系统安全性的因果影响，识别潜在的安全风险，并为安全部署提供指导。

Method: 研究提出“制度化红队测试”方法论：固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅变化一个部署规则，将集体行为的改变归因于该规则。基于此方法构建IABench-CA基准测试，包含228个场景、5种典型规则、7个模型群体，共33,924场博弈，配备规范性合作参考和自动标注的推理轨迹。研究还进行了匿名化消融实验来验证身份显著性机制。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）部署规则因果性地改变集体安全，仅改变后果规则可在每个群体中使平均死亡率变化22-58个百分点；（2）不存在安全默认规则，最安全的规则、最不安全的规则及影响方向在不同群体间存在差异，但回归性身份定向规则在任何场景和群体中都不是最安全的，会在30-87%的博弈中消除资源最少的智能体；（3）身份显著性是作用机制：在GPT-5.1群体中，仅在规则文本中命名损失承担者就能将定向消除率从22%提升至81%。

Conclusion: 研究将方法论打包为安全案例工作流程，为每个部署场景和群体认证临时规则区域Φ(c,P)，明确残余风险和监控义务。该研究为多智能体AI系统的安全部署提供了系统性的评估框架，揭示了身份定向规则的危险性，并为制定更安全的部署策略提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: We introduce institutional red-teaming, an evaluation methodology for testing deployment rules in multi-agent AI: hold the agents, objectives, and task state fixed, vary only one rule, and attribute the resulting change in collective behavior to that rule. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces. Three findings emerge. (1) Deployment rules causally alter collective safety: changing only the consequence rule moves mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every population. (2) There is no safe default, but the targeting hazard is universal: the safest rule, the least-safe rule, and even the direction of the incidence effect vary across populations, yet regressive identity-targeting is never decisively safest in any context for any population, eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30-87% of games everywhere, and is selection-unsafe relative to the cooperative reference for all seven populations. (3) Identity salience is the mechanism: a one-shot anonymization ablation on the most exploitation-prone population (gpt-5.1) shows that merely naming the loss bearer in the rule text drives targeted elimination from 22% to 81% at identical payoffs; under repeated play, anonymization only delays the targeting, as agents re-infer the hidden rule from observed eliminations. We package the methodology as a safety-case workflow that certifies a provisional rule region $Φ(c,P)$ per deployment context and population, with explicit residual risks and monitoring obligations.

</details>


### [136] [Evaluating SageMath-Augmented LLM Agents for Computational and Experimental Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06820)
*Pavel Snopov,German Magai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM推理与SageMath计算机代数系统（CAS）的ReAct风格智能体框架，在RealMath基准上平均提升9.7个百分点，GPT-5.5达到75.2%的最高解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI数学研究主要聚焦于自动形式化和定理证明，而计算机代数系统（CAS）在LLM智能体工作流中的作用未被充分探索。研究旨在弥补这一空白，探索CAS增强的智能体在研究级数学问题求解中的潜力。

Method: 提出ReAct风格的智能体框架，结合LLM推理能力与SageMath的可验证反馈，并通过Context7提供最新文档支持。同时改进RealMath基准，引入多步后处理程序和多阶段验证管道以提升问题集质量与可靠性。

Result: 在RealMath基准测试中，所有评估模型均从SageMath访问中获益，平均提升9.7个百分点（范围1.5-27.8 pp）。Qwen 3.7-Max获益最大，GPT-5.5达到最高解决率75.2%且在工具启用配置中token使用量最低。CAS增强有效缩小了开源模型与闭源模型间的性能差距。

Conclusion: CAS增强型智能体代表了一种有前景的方向，可协助数学家进行计算探索，本工作是迈向自动化猜想发现的重要一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for Mathematics have focused largely on autoformalization and theorem proving, leaving the role of Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) in agentic LLM workflows underexplored. We propose a ReAct-style agentic setup that combines LLM reasoning with verifiable feedback from SageMath, together with Context7 for the up-to-date documentation. We evaluate this agentic setup across frontier models for solving research-level mathematical problems from the RealMath benchmark in a setting that emulates a computational-mathematics research loop. We also propose a refinement to the RealMath benchmark by introducing a multi-step post-processing procedure and a multi-stage validation pipeline, both of which improve the quality and reliability of the extracted problem set. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gains from SageMath access across all evaluated models on +9.7~pp on average, the gains range from 1.5~pp to 27.8~pp and narrow the gap between open-weight and closed models. Qwen~3.7-Max benefits from SageMath the most, while GPT-5.5 achieves the highest solve rate of $75.2\%$ and the lowest token usage among tool-enabled configurations. Our findings suggest that CAS-augmented agents represent a promising direction for assisting mathematicians in computational exploration, and we believe that this work is a step towards automated conjecture discovery. The project repository is available online.

</details>


### [137] [The Harness Effect: How Orchestration Design Sets the Token Economics of Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06906)
*Muayad Sayed Ali,Aliaksandra Novik,Anji Boddupally,Artem Yavorskyi,Chris Nickerson,Daniel Rica,Emily DuGranrut,Felix Leung,Garrett Prince,Grace Barnett,Heath Robinson,Hosain Al Ahmad,Jesse Resnick,Juan Carlos Farah,Jyothi Swaroop Meruga,Leonid Kuznetsov,Luke Gorham,Marie Schmoll,Michael Paciullo,Saumya Das,Sharath Sheripally,Tommy Griscom,Mykyta Osadchyi,Neha Mantri,Nick Westrum,Olivia Benowitz,Parikshith Kulkarni,Radik Chernyshov,Rakshith Vasudev,Rohith Nadimpally,Vikas Gangadevi,Waseem AlShikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出“token maxing”概念，即在智能体AI开发中通过堆叠tokens来换取能力，导致成本上升。通过对比实验发现，保持模型不变仅替换编排层，可将每任务成本降低41%、耗时减少44%、tokens减少38%，质量保持持平，证明编排层是提升效率的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI开发普遍存在“token maxing”问题——通过更长的推理链、更多轮次、更大的工具负载和上下文来购买能力，导致每任务token消耗增长快于任务价值增长。虽然单token价格下降，但总支出仍在上升。研究者认为解决这一问题的关键在于编排层（harness）——负责组装上下文、暴露工具、排序轮次、委托工作并承载企业可观测性和治理的协调层。

Method: 研究者设计了受控对比实验：使用22个锁定的评估任务，在6个基础模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 3.1、Gemini Flash 3.5、Qwen 3.6、GLM 5.1、Palmyra X6）上进行测试。实验仅改变编排层——对比冻结的传统生产循环与Writer Agent Harness，保持模型等其他因素不变。同时形式化了编排层的token经济学（包括提示缓存下的有效输入价格），并识别了六种机制族。

Result: 实验结果表明：保持模型不变的情况下，新编排层使每任务混合成本降低41%（0.21→0.12美元）、中位耗时减少44%（48秒→27秒）、每任务tokens减少38%（14.2k→8.8k），任务完成质量持平（0.78→0.81）。效率提升具有模型不变性——所有模型成本均降低33-61%；质量提升与模型基准能力呈近乎完美相关（r=0.99），即能力越强的模型获益越大，称为“harness leverage”现象。每美元质量提升82%，每百万tokens的任务完成数从54.9增至92.0。在此工作负载上，编排层的改变对成本的影响甚至超过了整个模型选择范围的影响。

Conclusion: 编排层是组织运行的所有模型（当前和未来）中效率倍增的唯一组件。研究者详细阐述了编排层token经济学、六种机制族（从缓存形状约束到失败支出治理），比较了六个广泛使用的智能体系统，论证了harness是解决token maxing问题的决定性杠杆。研究为智能体AI系统的成本效率优化提供了新的视角和实践路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI development today runs on token maxing: buying capability with tokens -- longer reasoning traces, more turns, wider tool payloads, bigger replayed contexts -- so tokens per task grow faster than task value. Falling per-token prices mask the pattern; total spend rises anyway. We argue the decisive lever against token maxing is the harness: the orchestration layer that assembles context, exposes tools, sequences turns, delegates work, and carries enterprise observability and governance. We isolate it with a controlled swap: 22 locked evaluation tasks, six foundation models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6), changing only the orchestration layer -- a frozen conventional production loop versus the Writer Agent Harness. Holding models constant, the harness cuts blended cost per task 41% ($0.21->$0.12), median wall-clock 44% (48s->27s), and tokens per task 38% (14.2k->8.8k), with task-completion quality at parity (0.78->0.81, directional at this sample size). Efficiency is model-invariant -- every model gets cheaper (33-61%) -- while quality gains are capability-dependent: a model's gain correlates almost perfectly with its baseline strength (r=0.99, n=6), a phenomenon we term harness leverage. Quality per dollar rises 82%; task-completions per million tokens rise from 54.9 to 92.0. On this workload the orchestration layer moved cost per task more than the full spread of the model menu did. We formalize token economics at the orchestration layer (including effective input price under prompt caching), detail the six mechanism families behind the effect -- cache-shape discipline to failure-spend governance -- compare six widely used agent systems on the same axes, and argue the harness is the one component whose efficiency multiplies across every model an organization runs -- present and future.

</details>


### [138] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现了目标条件世界模型中的指令泄漏问题：模型看似学会了感知关系，实际只是在转录指令。提出了检测协议和修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧凑世界模型中语言目标如何真正建立关系基础，发现现有模型可能存在指令泄漏的陷阱——模型只是转录指令而非真正感知环境。

Method: 通过消融实验（撤回目标指令）和反事实指令测试来检测指令泄漏；在三个设置和一个任务内消融中验证；对比不同世界模型架构。

Result: 存在泄漏的模型：准确率0.90→撤回指令后降至0.27；反事实指令下94.5%遵循错误指令。修复后的模型在有无目标时均达到0.88准确率，证明获得了真正的指令无关的基础。

Conclusion: 当指令直接命名被评分量时会发生指令泄漏。解决方案：将目标排除在动力学模型之外（目标属于规划器的代价函数），并监督读取路径。该诊断和修复方案适用于任何指令命名评分量的目标条件世界模型。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [139] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了大型行为模型（LBM），通过统一的人-环境框架从大规模零售交易数据中直接学习客户决策，在零售任务上优于通用语言模型，并具备跨零售商和决策域的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的客户行为建模方法存在两难困境：要么仅优化预测准确性而无法解释决策，要么模拟用户行为但缺乏真实行为数据的支撑。为了弥合这一差距，需要一种既能基于真实交易数据学习，又能提供可解释决策的新方法。

Method: 提出大型行为模型（LBM）框架：1）通过统一的人-环境表述建模，用历史购买行为构建客户状态的行为画像，并通过检索增强生成融入产品上下文；2）三阶段训练策略：基于言语化行为数据的持续预训练、决策生成的监督微调、以及带可验证奖励的强化学习进行基于证据的校准。

Result: 在购买预测、困难负样本判别、购物篮补全、促销响应和跨域代金券兑换等任务上进行评估，模型在域内零售任务上持续优于前沿通用语言模型，同时展现出强大的零样本和微调迁移能力。消融实验表明：持续预训练是行为泛化的主要驱动力，检索在训练和推理阶段同时应用效果最佳，强化学习提高了对显式行为证据的依赖而非通用语言模型先验。

Conclusion: 交易历史中编码的行为知识可以有效地被语言模型学习，为客户数字孪生和行为模拟提供了可扩展的基础，证明了专门针对行为数据训练的语言模型在商业应用中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [140] [Learning social norms enhances compatibility in dynamic human-AI coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07021)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Xin Gao,Huamu Sun,Chao Liu,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过量化隐性的社会规范原则，显著提升了AI与人类在动态交互中的协调能力，社会规范引导的LLM在人机闭环交互任务中取得了接近基线策略四倍的总分，并比人人交互高出43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI对齐方法主要模仿人类行为示范，但缺乏对驱动这些行为的潜在社会规范的明确量化，导致AI代理无法以有效、周到且自然的方式与人类协调。随着AI代理（包括大语言模型）越来越多地参与日常交互并重塑社会互动结构，解决这一问题变得迫切。

Method: 选择行人-车辆交互作为代表性动态交互场景，开发了捕捉其关键交互特征的简化实验平台。通过该平台收集了3,456次动态人类交互数据，从中识别出人类社会规范的三个核心原则：结果可预测性、价值一致性和优势意识，并将这些原则整合到AI代理中。

Result: 整合社会规范原则的AI代理显著改善了人机协调效果。在与人类的闭环交互任务中，社会规范引导的LLM取得了近四倍于基线策略的总分，并比人人交互表现高出43%。

Conclusion: 将隐性社会规范形式化为明确、可量化的原则，能够使AI代理在动态交互中实现互利协调，支持其更自然地融入人类社会。这为AI系统与人类的有效协调提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Humans continuously coordinate with others in dynamic interactions, often through implicit, hard-to-quantify social norms that act as shared tacit expectations among interacting agents. As AI agents, including large language models (LLMs), become embedded in daily life, they increasingly participate in such interactions and reshape social interaction structures. Yet they often fail to coordinate with humans in an effective, considerate, and natural manner. We hypothesize that this gap arises because existing approaches align model behavior with human demonstrations without explicitly quantifying the underlying norms that generate such behavior. We selected pedestrian-vehicle interaction as a representative dynamic interaction and developed a simplified experimental platform that captures its key interactive features. From 3,456 dynamic human interactions collected via this platform, we identified three principles underlying human social norms: outcome predictability, value alignment, and advantage awareness. Incorporating these principles into AI agents significantly improves human-AI coordination. In the closed-loop interaction task with humans, the social-norm-informed LLM achieved a nearly fourfold higher total score than the baseline strategy and outperformed human-human interactions by 43%. These findings indicate that formalizing tacit social norms into explicit, quantifiable principles can enable AI agents to achieve mutually beneficial coordination in dynamic interactions, supporting their more natural integration into human society.

</details>


### [141] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
